#2320. Lotito verso la FIGC: per il voto del 14 dicembre servono i club

Tra i possibili consiglieri figurano Marotta, Campoccia e Chiellini; resta da definire il sostegno al presidente laziale.

Claudio Lotito punta a tornare nel Consiglio federale della FIGC e starebbe cercando il sostegno dei club di Serie A in vista del voto del 14 dicembre. Secondo quanto riportato da Calcio e Finanza, Il presidente della Lazio non fa parte dell’organismo da due anni. La sua eventuale candidatura si inserirebbe negli equilibri emersi dopo l’elezione di Giovanni Malagò alla guida federale.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Secondo la ricostruzione del Corriere dello Sport, Lotito vorrebbe tornare a incidere dall’interno sulle decisioni della FIGC. Per farlo, il senatore di Forza Italia avrebbe bisogno di raccogliere consensi tra le società della massima serie.

In passato il presidente biancoceleste aveva avuto un peso significativo nella Lega Serie A, ma il suo ruolo si è progressivamente ridimensionato. Il quotidiano riferisce che si sarebbe ritrovato isolato soprattutto dopo la frattura con alcuni club. Tra questi vengono indicati Inter e Atalanta, schierati su posizioni diverse dalle sue.

La ricerca di sostegno guarda all’appuntamento elettorale del 14 dicembre. Lotito potrebbe aver bisogno anche delle società che avevano contribuito all’elezione di Malagò, nonostante le distanze emerse negli ultimi mesi.

Quattro mesi fa i candidati per i 20 posti in Consiglio federale erano stati 21. Lotito era rimasto fuori dalla corsa, anche per la mancata presentazione della documentazione entro i termini previsti.

Tra i possibili consiglieri figurano Giuseppe Marotta per l’Inter, Stefano Campoccia per l’Udinese e Giorgio Chiellini per la Juventus. A loro potrebbe aggiungersi Lotito, che sarebbe stato coinvolto insieme a Saverio Sticchi Damiani del Lecce, indicato come uno dei principali sostenitori di Malagò.

#2318. Bristol Rovers vs Cambridge United - prediction, team news, lineups

By Calum Burrowes | 04 Nov 2026 14:45, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 14:53

The oldest football competition in the world gets underway later this week for clubs across the English Football League, with League Two side Bristol Rovers welcoming third-tier Cambridge United to the Memorial Stadium in the FA Cup first round.

The Gas are currently enduring a seven-game winless run in all competitions and face a U's side managed by Neil Harris, who saw his team thrash Brighton & Hove Albion Under-21s 6-0 in the EFL Trophy earlier this week.

Hoping to reach the second round of the FA Cup for the second consecutive campaign, Bristol Rovers head into Friday night's encounter without a victory since beating Exeter City 1-0 in September.

Steve Evans, who is approaching one year in charge of Bristol Rovers, guided the Gas to a mid-table finish last season after overseeing a nine-game unbeaten run to end the campaign, although they were unable to mount a serious challenge for the playoff places.

Their encouraging form towards the end of last season initially carried into the 2026-27 campaign, with Rovers sitting third in League Two at the end of September after winning five of their opening eight matches.

However, since closing out that month with a narrow victory over the Grecians, Evans's side have endured a miserable October, suffering defeats to Barnet, Shrewsbury Town, Salford City and Fleetwood Town.

That poor run has seen the Gas fall out of the automatic promotion places and slide to 17th in the table, with their 16 points leaving them just four clear of the relegation zone.

Returning to the Memorial Stadium for the first time since their goalless draw with Swindon Town, Rovers will hope some FA Cup magic can help end their three-game winless run at home and rediscover the form that saw them set a club-record 11 consecutive victories on their own turf.

As for Cambridge United, they enter the FA Cup as a League One side for the first time since 2024, having secured an immediate return to the third tier last season by finishing third in League Two with 82 points.

Under former Millwall and Gillingham manager Harris, the U's have adapted well to life back in League One and currently sit just one point outside the playoff places, having collected 21 points from five wins, six draws and three defeats.

Despite their encouraging start, Cambridge are without a league victory in four matches, with draws against Wycombe Wanderers and Sheffield Wednesday coming alongside defeats to Plymouth Argyle and Leyton Orient.

However, following their loss at Brisbane Road to close out October, the U's returned to winning ways in emphatic fashion earlier this week, securing progression from Southern Group G in the EFL Trophy with a 6-0 demolition of Brighton & Hove Albion Under-21s at Cledara Abbey Stadium where Callum Stead scored twice in the convincing victory, while the visitors also had a player sent off.

With no league fixture for more than two weeks, Harris will be keen for his side to build on that momentum by reaching the second round of the FA Cup before turning their attention back to League One, where Cambridge will also be looking to cut down on the number of draws that have hindered their progress, having shared the points more often than any other side in the division.

The sides last met in the FA Cup in December 1971, when the Pirates claimed a 3-0 victory over Cambridge in the second round.

Cambridge United form (all competitions):

Having faced a Fleetwood Town side who produced a shock victory over Arsenal in the EFL Cup just days earlier, Bristol Rovers could name an unchanged starting XI, provided there are no fresh injury concerns.

Captain Alfie Kilgour is expected to remain at the heart of a back three, flanked by Keenan Gough and Irish defender Ciaran Kelly.

Club top scorer Fabrizio Cavegn should lead the line, while Jack Sparkes is likely to retain his place in midfield.

Cambridge, meanwhile, are expected to make several changes to the side that featured in the EFL Trophy during the week, although Harris could still hand opportunities to fringe players against opposition from the division below.

Jake Eastwood has been the U's first-choice goalkeeper this season but could make way for 22-year-old Gabriel Breeze, who kept a clean sheet against Brighton's youth side on Tuesday night.

Ben Knight was absent from the squad for that EFL Trophy victory but could return to the attack, with former Barnet forward Stead expected to lead the line once again.

Young; Gough, Kilgour, Kelly; James, Smallwood, Mola, Sparkes; Forde, Cavegn, Appiah

Cambridge United possible starting lineup:

Breeze; Heffernan, Watts, Perry; Bennett, Hoddle, McLoughlin, Bradshaw; Knight, Stead, Heath

Although neither side enjoyed a particularly impressive October, Cambridge will take confidence from their emphatic EFL Trophy victory earlier this week.

We expect the difference in league positions to prove decisive on Friday night, with the visitors securing a comfortable win to progress to the second round of the FA Cup.

#2317. Juve Stabia, De Giorgio: "Gli episodi ci sono costati punti, dobbiamo imparare"

Pietro De Giorgio, tecnico della Juve Stabia, ha parlato in conferenza stampa alla viglia della sfida con l'Arezzo.

"È una partita che abbiamo preparato con tanta attenzione. Serviranno grande qualità, attenzione mentale e pazienza. Sono tre punti fondamentali, perché dobbiamo allontanarci definitivamente da una zona di classifica pericolosa nella quale ci troviamo per una situazione che ci portiamo dietro dall’inizio del campionato. I punti che abbiamo conquistato sul campo raccontano un’altra realtà, ma dobbiamo fare i conti con la classifica attuale. I ragazzi stanno bene e abbiamo lavorato bene: vogliamo chiudere il ciclo di partite casalinghe prima della sosta con un risultato positivo".

"L’Arezzo fuori casa ha vinto tre partite, mentre tra le mura amiche sta incontrando qualche difficoltà in più. Ha un atteggiamento che mette in difficoltà le squadre che giocano in casa. Sarà una gara complicata contro una squadra attenta quando non è in possesso e molto fastidiosa quando riparte. Dovremo avere qualità, pazienza e soprattutto maturità".

Quanto è importante imparare dagli episodi recenti?

"Gli episodi che ci sono costati punti devono diventare motivi di crescita, sono situazioni che non dobbiamo ripetere. Sono convinto che questa squadra farà bene: vedo quotidianamente gli allenamenti, la crescita dei ragazzi e le proposte che riescono a mettere in pratica. Sono molto contento del percorso di miglioramento complessivo".

Come sta Vismara? Vukovic sarà ancora titolare?

"Vismara non ha ancora recuperato completamente. Ha fatto quattro giorni con la squadra e non è ancora arruolabile. Dobbiamo riportarlo nelle condizioni ottimali e poi valutarlo. È sicuramente positivo avere tre portieri validi, ma in questo momento Vukovic sta facendo molto bene ed è giusto dargli continuità e serenità. Si è conquistato il posto e attualmente è lui il titolare".

Come valuta il rendimento del reparto offensivo?

"I tre davanti stanno facendo molto bene, anche se sono convinto che abbiamo ancora tanto da migliorare. È un attacco che ci garantisce qualità, fisicità, capacità di attaccare la profondità e di giocare tra le linee. In questo momento è un reparto che ci completa".

"Mi dispiace molto per la sua assenza, perché lo considero un giocatore forte e poteva rappresentare un valore aggiunto".

Caccavo può giocare insieme a Candellone? Come stanno crescendo Fernandes e Patanè?

"Caccavo sta facendo molto bene ed è pronto per darci una mano. Stiamo lavorando anche sulla possibilità di utilizzare due attaccanti insieme. Sanà Fernandes sta entrando sempre più nei meccanismi della squadra: a Empoli l’ho impiegato a destra, un ruolo al quale non era abituato, e stiamo alternandolo sulle due corsie. Anche Patanè sta lavorando per adattarsi a una posizione più avanzata, perché cambiano la postura, il primo controllo e i tempi per attaccare la profondità".

Di Nardo sta segnando molto. Come può migliorare la squadra nella fase offensiva?

"Di Nardo è arrivato con un’umiltà incredibile e ci ha dato la possibilità di lavorare ulteriormente sul suo modo di attaccare la porta. Lo sta facendo con entusiasmo e molto bene. È un giocatore che viene messo nelle condizioni di tirare tante volte anche grazie ai compagni, ma ha qualità importanti e calcia davvero bene.

Dobbiamo migliorare con il resto del gruppo, comprese le mezzali, soprattutto nella qualità dell’ultimo passaggio e nelle conclusioni. Siamo ultimi in classifica per occasioni create e occasioni sfruttate, quindi dobbiamo aumentare la qualità delle situazioni che riusciamo a trasformare in gol. È un aspetto che finora ci ha fatto perdere anche qualche punto".

Il sintetico del Menti può rappresentare un vantaggio?

"Il nostro sintetico è molto simile all’erba naturale, sia per quanto riguarda l’appoggio sia per la pesantezza del terreno. Credo che questo sia stato uno dei motivi per cui quest’anno abbiamo fatto poca fatica ad adattarci ai campi in erba quando abbiamo giocato in trasferta. È una caratteristica che potrebbe favorire anche le squadre che vengono a giocare qui".

Come si aspetta che l'Arezzo interpreti la partita?

"In possesso affrontiamo una squadra che gioca a specchio, ma in fase di non possesso si adatta molto all’avversario. È una formazione che difende bene e che può mettersi anche con cinque uomini dietro. Noi dovremo essere bravi a leggere queste situazioni e prestare particolare attenzione ai loro due esterni, molto abili nell’uno contro uno e capaci di metterti in difficoltà in qualsiasi momento".

La Juve Stabia può cambiare modulo in base agli interpreti?

"Lo stiamo già facendo. Se gioca Patanè abbiamo un determinato modo di giocare, con Pierobon possiamo avere un altro atteggiamento, così come con Kumi. Cerchiamo di modellare il nostro sistema sulle qualità dei calciatori. Anche in fase offensiva stiamo lavorando per avere più soluzioni e permettere, quando necessario, di giocare con due attaccanti". Le sue parole riportate da vicicentro.

#2316. Gennaro Gattuso premiato Allenatore del Mese di ottobre

Il tecnico biancoceleste vince il premio di Allenatore del Mese di ottobre assegnato dalla Lega Serie A

Dopo 10 giornate la Lazio di Gennaro Gattuso si trova in testa alla classifica in solitaria con ben 28 punti a fronte dei 30 disponibili. Gli ultimi 30 giorni sostanzialmente perfetti sono valsi al tecnico biancoceleste il premio di Allenatore del Mese di ottobre assegnato dalla Lega Serie A.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il premio Philadelphia Coach Of The Month di ottobre è stato assegnato all’allenatore della Lazio Gennaro Gattuso. La consegna del trofeo avverrà nel pre-partita di Lazio-Lecce, in programma sabato 21 novembre alle ore 15.00 allo stadio “Olimpico” di Roma.

Il premio è stato assegnato da una giuria composta da Direttori di testate giornalistiche sportive che hanno valutato i singoli allenatori in base a criteri tecnico sportivi e di qualità di gioco espresso dalle loro squadre.

Per il calcolo finale sono state considerate le giornate dalla 6ª alla 9ª della Serie A Enilive 2025/2026.

“Il primato solitario in classifica con il record di punti dopo 10 giornate nella storia della Lazio raccontano l’impatto di Rino Gattuso sulla panchina biancoceleste - ha commentato l’Amministratore Delegato di Lega Calcio Serie A Luigi De Siervo -. Gattuso ha costruito una squadra moderna dotata di organizzazione, intensità, e soprattutto capace di segnare con dodici giocatori diversi e di difendere con la mentalità di chi non vuole concedere nulla. Al di là del valore sportivo, va riconosciuto al tecnico calabrese il grande merito di aver trasmesso ai suoi ragazzi il proprio spirito combattivo, plasmando un gruppo unito che ha sa adattarsi al meglio ai vari momenti delle partite”.

#2315. Venezia vs Udinese - prediction, team news, lineups

By Jonathan O'Shea | 04 Nov 2026 15:04, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 15:06

The Serie A weekend will kick off with a battle at the bottom under Friday night lights, as Udinese visit Venezia at Stadio Penzo.

After Kosta Runjaic was sacked earlier this week, both clubs have already changed manager due to a truly dismal set of results.

Promoted back to Italy’s top flight last spring, Venezia have only picked up three points since returning to Serie A, and they are rooted to the foot of the table.

After a slow start, the Lagunari swapped Giovanni Stroppa for survival specialist Davide Nicola, whose new side impressively upset Napoli in his first match.

However, they have since lost their next three games without scoring; last weekend, they were beaten 1-0 by Como, despite the best efforts of busy goalkeeper Filip Stankovic.

As a result, Venezia are only the third team ever to lose at least nine of their first 10 Serie A fixtures, leaving Nicola with issues to fix at both ends.

Not only have the Arancioneroverdi scored just six league goals, they are currently conceding at a rate of two per game - undoubtedly relegation form.

Now, they will host opponents in similarly dire shape; as Udinese are just two points above them, victory would lift Venezia off the bottom rung.

Most date back several years, but Udinese have won five of the sides' last seven league meetings, and they came out on top in a Coppa Italia tie earlier this term.

However, they have only ever posted one top-flight victory at Stadio Penzo, and the Fruilani are currently in freefall.

After a 5-2 crushing by Roma last week, Udinese have lost seven of their last eight matches, and that heavy home defeat proved the final stand for Runjaic.

The German coach had fared well in his first two years at the club, but a tally of five points from 10 Serie A games represents their worst start this century.

Without missing a beat, ex-Venezia boss Paolo Zanetti was appointed until the end of the season, and he must first visit his former club.

Since getting the Lagunari promoted for the first time in 19 years, Zanetti's personal record has been mixed elsewhere, so he will surely have a point to prove on Friday.

Marin Sverko, Bartol Franjic and Andrea Adorante are still ruled out for Venezia, while wing-back Thierry Correia is struggling to recover from a thigh strain.

After Lion Lauerbach started the Lagunari's last two matches, it remains to be seen if John Yeboah reforms his partnership with club-record signing Akor Adams.

Only Roma star Donyell Malen (15) has had more shots on target than Adams (13) in Serie A this term, but the Nigerian striker has only scored twice - both times on home turf.

Meanwhile, Udinese's main man is enduring a much leaner run: since the end of March, Keinan Davis has gone 13 league games without finding the net.

Attacking midfielder Jurgen Ekkelenkamp is the Friulani's top Serie A scorer with three goals, and he should certainly feature in Zanetti's first XI.

Although Oumar Solet has overcome a calf problem, the visitors will be without Alessandro Zanoli, David Alaba and Idrissa Gueye; the matchwinner when these teams recently clashed in the cup, Vakoun Bayo remains a doubt.

Stankovic; Halhal, Moreno, Jesus; Hainaut, Perez, Busio, Sohm, Mazzocchi; Yeboah, Adams

Okoye; Abankwah, Solet, Palma; Vojvoda, Karlstrom, Piotrowski, Kamara; Zaniolo, Ekkelenkamp; Davis

With the worst goal difference and fewest points in Serie A, this pair have been in desperate form for most of the season.

Perhaps a 'new manager bounce' can help Udinese finally stir from their malaise, as Zanetti makes a triumphant return to the Penzo.

#2313. FIGC, Malagò: “Rispetto e stimo Galliani, lui focalizzato sul calcio, io porto idee e visioni anche da fuori”

“La mia ricandidatura? Mi sembrava doveroso dopo essere stato eletto il 22 giugno con una maggioranza importante, dopo tutto quello che è accaduto tra pantouflage e tesseramento, dopo aver difeso l’autonomia del calcio. Non è un tema anagrafico né di capacità, ma un percorso diverso della nostra storia: lui molto focalizzato sul calcio, io penso di portare idee e visioni che vengono anche da fuori, pur conoscendo bene il calcio”. Se fossimo andati al voto in quella giunta ne saremmo usciti tutti sconfitti, non so se è stata la soluzione migliore, ma sicuramente la meno peggio. Roma 2024? La ferita resterà per sempre, una scelta scellerata che portò disastri in termini di consenso anche a chi la prese. Ma dopo sei mesi ci ritrovammo a occuparci di Milano Cortina, e quindi. Latina fu fortemente penalizzata da quella decisione. Avremmo vinto e coinvolto questa zona come abbiamo fatto con tanti territori per Milano Cortina”.

#2311. Sevilla vs Alaves - prediction, team news, lineups

By Matt Law | 04 Nov 2026 15:53, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 15:58

Sevilla will be aiming to return to winning ways in Spain's top flight when they welcome Alaves to Estadio Ramon on Friday evening.

The home team are currently sixth in the La Liga table, picking up 17 points from 11 matches, while Alaves are 11th in the division, claiming 12 points from 11 games.

Sevilla boast a record of five wins, two draws and four defeats from their 11 league matches this season, with 17 points leaving them in a European spot in the division, sitting sixth.

It has been a difficult period of the season for Luis Garcia Plaza's side, who have only been victorious in one of their last five matches in Spain's top flight.

Sevilla will enter this game off the back of a 3-1 defeat to Getafe, with their last success coming in their last home league fixture - a 2-0 victory over Osasuna.

Los Nervionenses finished 13th in Spain's top flight last season, but they have the look of a team capable of challenging for a European finish this term.

Sevilla have won 21 of their previous 45 matches against Alaves, suffering 12 defeats in the process, but it was 1-1 when the pair last locked horns in February 2026.

Alaves have only actually lost one of their last seven matches against Sevilla, recording four wins in the process, so the visitors should enter this game in a confident mood.

The Blue and Whites suffered a 6-1 defeat to Barcelona last time out, and a tough run of form has seen them pick up just two points from their last seven matches.

Alaves have not been victorious in the league since the start of September, when they recorded a 5-2 win over Osasuna, and their recent struggles have seen them drop down the table, currently sitting 11th with 12 points from 11 matches.

Quique Sanchez Flores' side are just two points off the relegation zone but also only three points behind seventh-placed Athletic Bilbao, so a lot could change with a victory in this match.

Alaves are enjoying a strong period in their history given that nine of their last 10 seasons have been played in the top flight of Spanish football.

Sevilla will be without the services of Manu Bueno, with the midfielder sent off in the team's 3-1 defeat to Getafe last time out.

Bueno had actually been substituted into the match for Isaac Romero, who suffered an injury, and the striker is regarded as a major doubt for this contest.

There will also be absences for Marcao and Miguel Sierra, with the pair unavailable due to muscular and shoulder injuries respectively.

As for Alaves, Facundo Garces and Mikel Rodriguez are both out of the match through injury, but the visitors did not suffer any fresh issues against Barcelona.

Head coach Sanchez Flores is expected to resist the temptation to shuffle his pack despite the disappointing nature of the result against the La Liga champions.

Toni Martinez is expected to be introduced back into the side, while there will be a spot in the final third of the field for Lucas Boye, who has netted five league goals this term.

#2308. Oxford City vs Leicester City - prediction, team news, lineups

By Matt Law | 04 Nov 2026 16:25, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 22:11

Fallen giants Leicester City will be aiming to avoid what would be one of the biggest shocks in recent FA Cup history when they head to National League North outfit Oxford City on Friday night in the first round of the tournament.

It is the first time that Leicester have competed in this round since 2008, and Oxford City have been given the chance to claim a huge scalp in the tournament.

Oxford City have managed to progress to the second round of the FA Cup on three previous occasions - 1969-70, 2017-18 and 2020-21, so the club have previous when it comes to advancing past this stage of the famous cup competition.

The Hoops will enter this match off the back of a disappointing result, though, going down 3-1 at home to South Shields in the National League North on Saturday.

Ross Jenkins' side are currently second in the National League North, picking up 27 points from their opening 14 matches of the campaign, two points behind South Shields following the clash between the top two.

Oxford City have had to win three matches to reach this stage of the FA Cup, overcoming Dorchester, Enfield Town and Torquay to be present in the first round.

Leicester, meanwhile, will enter this match off the back of a 2-2 draw with Stevenage, with the result leaving them in 10th spot in the League One table.

The Foxes have won three of their last five league games, but there is no getting away from the fact that it has been a disappointing start to the season as a whole.

Leicester famously won this competition back in 2021, recording a 1-0 victory over Chelsea courtesy of a second-half effort from Youri Tielemans.

Russell Martin is still under pressure at Leicester but remains at the helm, with the 40-year-old bidding to help the Foxes secure a return to the Championship.

Leicester's last FA Cup fixture brought a 2-1 defeat to Southampton in the last-16 stage of the 2025-26 competition.

Oxford City will be looking to Tyrone Marsh for goals on Friday, with the 32-year-old netting nine times in all competitions at the start of the 2026-27 campaign.

There will also be a position in the team for Scott Pollock, who has been another key performer this season, coming up with six goals and two assists in 13 appearances.

Ludwig Francillette and Josh Ashby are also set to be notable starters.

As for Leicester, there are set to be a number of changes to the team that took to the field for the 2-2 draw with Stevenage in the league last time out.

However, Ben Nelson and Jakub Stolarczyk are both out of the match through injury.

Conor Chaplin should be introduced in the final third of the field, while 19-year-old Ilari Kangasniemi is expected to be a notable starter at the back.

Fellow teenager Louis Page is expected to be among those to be rested.

We are not predicting an FA Cup shock on Friday night; Leicester should have too much quality for their National League North opponents in the first round of the tournament.

#2305. Gaziantep vs Kasimpasa - prediction, team news, lineups

By Joshua Cole | 04 Nov 2026 16:42, Last updated: 06 Nov 2026 07:16

Gaziantep will be desperate to halt their alarming slide when they welcome Kasimpasa to Gaziantep Stadyumu on Friday in the Turkish Super Lig.

The hosts have lost five consecutive league matches and are hovering just above the relegation zone with eight points from their opening 10 fixtures, while their visitors occupy fifth place with 17 points and arrive unbeaten in their last three league outings, with a new manager at the helm.

Gaziantep have endured a miserable spell in the league, with their latest setback coming in a 2-0 defeat away to Trabzonspor.

Orhan Ak's side struggled to make an impact against the Black Sea outfit, conceding once in each half as they suffered another damaging result following defeats against Erzurumspor, Istanbul Basaksehir, Corum and Kocaelispor.

Their 4-0 Turkish Cup victory over Etimesgutspor provided some relief, but that result has done little to disguise their domestic difficulties, particularly as they have scored just twice across their last five league matches while conceding 12 goals.

The lack of attacking output is becoming a significant concern for Ak, whose team have struggled to turn possession into meaningful opportunities and have repeatedly found themselves chasing matches after falling behind.

Gaziantep have also struggled to establish Gaziantep Stadyumu as a source of points, failing to win any game as hosts yet, drawing two and losing three times in five home matches.

However, the Falcons are unbeaten in their last five meetings with the Apaches, picking up three wins and two draws, while they have also avoided defeat in their last three encounters against them as hosts, winning twice and drawing once.

Kasimpasa, meanwhile, have enjoyed a considerably more encouraging opening to the campaign, losing just one of their 10 league games so far while recording four wins and five draws.

The Apaches were held to an entertaining 2-2 draw by Besiktas in their previous league outing, with Claudio Winck and Michael Rafferty finding the net as they twice threatened to take all three points.

That result extended their unbeaten league sequence to three matches, following victories over Eyupspor and Samsunspor, while they also recorded a 3-1 win over Corluspor in the Turkish Cup, extending their unbeaten run across all competitions to four matches.

Kasimpasa have also collected useful points on their travels this season, recording 10 points from five away games, with three wins, one draw and one defeat, which should give them optimism ahead of this trip.

However, there is also an air of uncertainty around the club after losing Emre Belozoglu to Fenerbahce, with Alfred Schreuder appointed as the new manager and heading into his first game at the club.

Gaziantep have several availability concerns ahead of Friday's encounter, with Myenty Abena facing suspension following a yellow-card accumulation, while Ulrich Meleke, Ogun Ozcicek, Arda Kizildag and Sontje Hansen are among those carrying injuries.

Serdar Dursun is also expected to miss out, while Nazim Sangare and Fuat Bavuk remain unavailable.

Kasimpasa have fewer confirmed concerns, although Kamil Ahmet Corekci is expected to remain sidelined with an Achilles tendon injury.

Godfried Frimpong and Kevin Mouanga are also carrying fitness problems, leaving the visitors potentially short of defensive options, while no further suspensions have been reported.

Adrian Benedyczak has scored six goals in the league this season, and he will be the man to watch for the Apaches in this encounter.

Dadakdeniz; Perez, Abena, Sylla, Calhanoglu; Bacuna; Sorescu, Gidado, Maxim, Kozlowski; Stewart

Gianniotis; Jensen, Ilie, Arous, Winck; Demirbay, Mendes; Hajradinovic, Rak-Sakyi, Kol; Benedyczak

Gaziantep's home advantage and historical record in this fixture could make Friday's encounter more competitive than the league standings suggest, but their current defensive problems are difficult to overlook.

Kasimpasa have shown considerably more attacking consistency, and their recent form should ultimately see them claim all three points.

Ivan Provedel ha raccontato il suo trasferimento estivo dalla Lazio all’Inter nella quarta puntata di Welcome Kit, il format di Inter TV powered by Betsson Sport. Il portiere ha parlato della chiamata ricevuta dai nerazzurri, dell’obiettivo che si è dato nella nuova squadra e del ricordo di Toldo, il suo idolo.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Il contatto dell’Inter ha colto Provedel di sorpresa: «Quando mi ha chiamato l’Inter sono stato un po’ incredulo». A quella prima reazione non è seguita subito la certezza del trasferimento. Il portiere ha spiegato di aver aspettato che l’operazione fosse conclusa prima di lasciarsi andare all’entusiasmo: «fino a che non ho firmato non ho voluto pensare o illudermi, fino a che non è stato tutto fatto». Solo dopo la firma, ha raccontato, è stato felicissimo.

Provedel ha indicato anche quale sia il suo primo obiettivo da interista: «Dimostrare che qui ci posso stare e che posso dare il mio contributo alla squadra». Il portiere vuole quindi mostrare di poter far parte dell’Inter e aiutare i compagni. Alla domanda su che cosa rappresenti per lui indossare la maglia del club, ha risposto: «Un sogno che si realizza».

Nelle sue parole, l’attesa prudente prima della firma lascia spazio alla felicità per il passaggio all’Inter. Provedel ha collegato l’arrivo in nerazzurro al desiderio di dare un contributo alla squadra, oltre che al significato personale della maglia che ora indossa.

#2298. Sparta Prague vs. Bournemouth: How to watch Europa League fixture, date, time, live stream and TV channel

By Ben Sully | 04 Nov 2026 17:03, Last updated: 05 Nov 2026 03:26

Bournemouth will continue their maiden European campaign in Thursday's meeting with Sparta Prague in the Europa League league phase.

The Cherries are sitting in third place in the Europa League standings, while the hosts start matchday four in 16th position.

Here, Sports Mole provides all the key details about how to tune into the European fixture.

The game will get underway at 18:45pm local time on Thursday, which will be a 17:45 kick-off for UK viewers.

Bournemouth will travel to Stadion Letna, which can hold over 18,000 spectators.

Sparta have won six of their nine competitive home matches this season (D1, L2), although they have failed to win two of their last three games in front of their supporters.

UK viewers with the required subscription can watch the game on TNT Sports 2.

Alternatively, you can catch the game on the HBO Max website and app with a TNT Sports subscription. The HBO Max app is available on a wide range of devices, including phones, tablets, smart TVs and game consoles.

There is also the option to purchase a TNT Sports subscription as an add-on on Amazon Prime Video.

There will be match highlights available on the HBO Max website and app, as well as the TNT Sports Football YouTube channel.

The @footballontnt X account will also share key moments from Thursday's contest.

After featuring in the Conference League last 16 last term, Sparta are aiming to make it back-to-back European knockout appearances in this season's Europa League.

Brian Priske's side are currently sitting in a seeded spot for the knockout round playoffs, with three points separating them from the top eight and a one-point gap to the teams sitting just outside the top 24.

In regard to their overall form, Sparta will be looking to spark an upturn in fortunes after winning just one of their last six competitive matches.

In contrast to their hosts, Bournemouth carry significant momentum into the midweek fixture after winning each of their last six competitive matches without conceding, including a 2-0 Premier League win over Leeds United on Saturday.

Marco Rose's side are looking to maintain their 100% Europa League record after starting their European campaign with three successive wins over Real Sociedad, Sturm Graz and AC Milan.

Another win on Thursday would further strengthen their hopes of a top-eight finish and an automatic last-16 spot.

#2296. JJ Gabriel future: Man United's stance 'revealed' amid reports of Real Madrid 'agreement'

By Matt Law | 04 Nov 2026 17:12, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 17:15

Manchester United are reportedly in the dark when it comes to JJ Gabriel's future despite reports that the attacker has agreed a move to Real Madrid.

Widely regarded as one of the best teenagers in world football, JJ Gabriel stunned Man United in September when he told the club that he wanted to leave.

JJ Gabriel made his senior debut for Man United in their pre-season game against Atletico Madrid during the summer, but he is yet to feature for the first team in a competitive match and is now set to leave the 20-time English champions.

The 16-year-old had been due to sign scholarship terms with the Red Devils last month, with a professional contract then expected to follow a year later.

However, JJ Gabriel's representatives told Man United in September that the attacker would not be signing a new contract and wanted to leave.

According to Sky Sports News, Real Madrid have reached a verbal agreement with JJ Gabriel over a move to Bernabeu in January.

JJ Gabriel has a European passport through both of his parents, and a FIFA loophole could see him join Real Madrid in January despite having a deal with Man United's academy until the end of the campaign.

The attacker is currently in Spain maintaining his fitness, and Barcelona have also been heavily linked with a player regarded as one of the best teenagers in world football.

However, various reports have claimed that Real Madrid are closing in on a move.

According to the Manchester Evening News, Man United have had no contact from JJ Gabriel's representatives nor Real Madrid despite the reports.

Man United still hold JJ Gabriel's registration, but the teenager has not been at Carrington since scoring a hat-trick against Sabah in the UEFA Youth League at the start of September.

JJ Gabriel boasts a record of 29 goals and four assists in 32 appearances for Man United Under-18s. while he has scored twice and registered two assists in two appearances for Man United Under-21s in the Premier League 2.

Man United had allegedly initially been hopeful of keeping JJ Gabriel but have since admitted defeat in what is a major blow for the 20-time English champions.

#2292. Bruno Fernandes admits Michael Carrick was right to drop him

By Naim Beneddra | 04 Nov 2026 17:54

Manchester United captain Bruno Fernandes has admitted that Michael Carrick was justified in dropping him from the starting lineup, while denying reports of a breakdown in his relationship with the under-pressure manager.

The Portugal international made the surprising admission following United's dramatic 3-2 Champions League victory over Roma on Tuesday, which ended a nine-match winless run and provided some much-needed relief at Old Trafford.

Fernandes had been left on the bench for last month's Premier League defeat to Bournemouth, just days after publicly questioning the team's tactical approach following a humiliating 4-1 loss to Como.

His comments prompted speculation that Carrick had punished his captain for speaking out, although the United boss maintained that the decision had been made for footballing reasons.

Now, Fernandes has acknowledged that his own performances warranted the decision, while insisting that the controversy had not affected his confidence in the former England midfielder.

"Carrick was right" ?? Bruno Fernandes gave an open, honest assessment after United's 3-2 win over Roma ?️ https://t.co/QAWDt6VXQr

Speaking to Amazon Prime Video after the Roma victory, Fernandes addressed the circumstances surrounding his absence from the starting lineup against Bournemouth.

The 32-year-old revealed that he understood why Carrick had decided to make a change, even though the timing had led to suggestions of a disciplinary response.

"The manager took a decision of putting me on the bench because I wasn't performing," Fernandes admitted.

"Everyone felt that was a punishment for the words that I had. If it was, it was right."

Those comments represent a notable change in tone from the midfielder, whose public criticism after the Como defeat had intensified scrutiny of Carrick's management.

Fernandes had questioned United's decision not to press their opponents man-to-man and suggested that the players needed to show greater bravery under pressure.

Four days later, he was named among the substitutes for the 3-0 defeat to Bournemouth, marking the first time Carrick had dropped him from a Premier League starting lineup.

At the time, the manager insisted that his decision to leave Fernandes out was based purely on footballing considerations.

The captain's latest explanation supports that assessment, although he stopped short of confirming whether his earlier comments had influenced Carrick's thinking.

Fernandes also challenged reports suggesting that he and the manager had needed to resolve a personal disagreement behind the scenes.

He described some of the speculation surrounding their relationship as "lies", rejecting claims that the pair had held talks to clear the air following his controversial interview.

The midfielder was equally clear that being dropped had not diminished his commitment to Carrick or the club.

"It doesn't change the belief I have in the manager," Fernandes insisted, adding that he retained the same confidence in the coaching staff and his teammates.

His response is significant given the uncertainty surrounding Carrick's position and the questions that have been raised about the mood within United's dressing room.

Rather than portraying his omission as an injustice, Fernandes has publicly accepted responsibility for his performances and acknowledged the manager's authority to make difficult selection decisions.

Fernandes also responded to suggestions that United's remarkable recovery against Roma might have saved Carrick from dismissal.

The Red Devils had entered Tuesday's encounter without a victory in nine matches across all competitions, having slipped to 17th in the Premier League.

Their situation appeared to deteriorate further when Matias Soule and Donyell Malen gave Roma a two-goal advantage in the opening stages at Old Trafford.

However, Fernandes converted a retaken penalty in the second half before Matheus Cunha scored twice late on to complete the comeback.

Asked afterwards whether the players had rescued their manager's job, the United captain rejected that interpretation.

"We don't need to save anyone's job," Fernandes declared.

He explained that the victory had been necessary for the entire club after a prolonged period of poor results, rather than solely for Carrick's personal circumstances.

The Portuguese also acknowledged the frustration felt by supporters, who have watched United struggle to reproduce the standards associated with the club's most successful years.

Carrick adopted a similarly measured approach in his post-match comments, describing the victory as an important moment for the club without suggesting that one result had resolved its problems.

United's next test comes against Aston Villa at Old Trafford on Sunday, November 8, when they will attempt to secure consecutive victories for the first time in several weeks.

Fernandes has already returned to Carrick's starting lineup, featuring against Chelsea before playing a decisive role in the European comeback.

His latest comments may help dispel some of the speculation surrounding his relationship with the manager. Whether United can build on Tuesday's result will provide a more meaningful indication of Carrick's prospects of remaining in charge.

#2291. Famalicao vs Arouca - prediction, team news, lineups

By Adeyeye Oluwapelumi | 04 Nov 2026 18:51

Seaching for their first home victory this season, Famalicao welcome an in-form Arouca side to the Estadio Municipal 22 de Junho on Friday night.

The hosts sit 11th in the Primeira Liga table, three points above the relegation zone, while the visitors occupy fifth place, ahead of sixth-placed Academico de Viseu on goal difference.

Famalicao have struggled to reproduce the form that earned them a club-best fifth-place finish last season, with the departures of former boss Hugo Oliveira, Ibrahima Ba and Gustavo Sa contributing to a difficult start to the new campaign.

Carlos Carvalhal, who took over in the summer, was dismissed in October following a shock Taca de Portugal defeat to third-tier Paredes, leaving 46-year-old assistant coach Ricardo Silva in charge on an interim basis.

Although Silva made an impressive start, securing a 4-0 away victory over Rio Ave before edging Braga on penalties in the Taca da Liga quarter-finals, the Vila Nova outfit were brought back down to earth by the same Minho rivals at the weekend, suffering a 3-0 league defeat.

That result leaves Famalicao with just two wins from their opening 10 Primeira Liga fixtures this season (D4, L4), while they have scored 14 goals and conceded the same number, highlighting the need for greater efficiency at both ends of the pitch.

The Vila Nova outfit have also struggled at the Estadio Municipal 22 de Junho this term, with Fama among three sides in the division yet to record a home victory, having collected just two points from four outings on their own turf, the most recent of which was a 4-1 defeat to Alverca.

Arouca, on the other hand, arrive in considerably better shape compared to the hosts, having enjoyed an impressive start under Vitor Severino, who took charge in October following Vasco Seabra's departure for Rio Ave.

Although the Wolves had enjoyed an encouraging campaign under Seabra, Severino inherited a side that had gone three matches without a win but has since guided them to four consecutive victories across the league and Taca de Portugal.

An extra-time cup victory over Belenenses came between three successive 1-0 league wins against Estrela Amadora, Alverca and Nacional, leaving Arouca with 13 goals scored and seven conceded in their opening 10 Primeira Liga matches, as well as a joint-league-high six clean sheets.

The Wolves have also been resilient on their travels, with their only defeat in five league trips this season coming against defending champions Porto, while two victories and two draws have given them eight points and made them the division's eighth-best away side.

However, Arouca have struggled in recent meetings with Famalicao, going winless in their last four competitive encounters and losing two of their previous three league visits to Vila Nova, while failing to score during that period.

Famalicao will be without Rodrigo Pinheiro, who is suspended after being sent off during last weekend's league defeat to Braga.

Georgios Koutsias is expected to lead the attack after scoring five league goals this season, while Sorriso and Romeo Beney could provide support from the flanks, with Oscar Aranda another option in the wide areas.

Arouca have a considerably cleaner bill of health, with Miguel Puche their only notable absentee as he continues his recovery from an anterior cruciate ligament injury.

Gabriel Rukavina is available again after serving a one-match suspension following his dismissal against Alverca, although the midfielder is yet to start a league game since joining from Rijeka in the summer.

Ivan Barbero should lead the line as he looks to add to his five league goals this season, while Nais Djouahra is expected to feature prominently after registering five assists in the competition.

Carevic; Garcia, Rofino, Realpe, Bondo; Van de Looi, De Amorim; Beney, Santos, Sorriso; Koutsias.

De Arruabarrena; Esgaio, Sanchez, Fontan, Lebedenko; Van Ee, Fukui; Trezza, Flores, Djouahra; Barbero.

Famalicao's home struggles and the uncertainty surrounding their interim management make it difficult to back them against an Arouca side that has found its rhythm and been particularly solid defensively, so a narrow away victory looks likely.

#2290. Cardinale: “Milan were at the top of the world, and we’re going to bring them back there”

AC Milan owner Gerry Cardinale spoke to CBS Sports before the derby win over Inter, pledging to bring the club back to the summit of football.

On Halloween night, the Rossoneri ensured that Inter’s derby nightmare continued as Milan won their fourth consecutive victory in the fixture, extending the unbeaten run against the Nerazzurri to eight meetings in the process.

Read SempreMilan ad-free and get access to exclusive news. Click here for a free trial!

All of the damage was done in the first half. Christian Pulisic struck in the fourth minute with a header, Adrien Rabiot scored from a corner six minutes later, Alessandro Bastoni halved the deficit and then Diego Moreira converted a lovely free-kick to reopen the two-goal advantage.

In the end, 3-1 is how it would stay, securing a memorable win. Cardinale spoke with the CBS panel in the build-up to kick-off at San Siro last night and he shed a bit more light on his plans for the Rossoneri moving forward. His words are via MilanNews.

“Well, first of all, this stadium… As an American, I can say that a stadium like this is something you never see anywhere else. Tonight, it’s going to light up. There’s something different about this derby tonight than in the past.

“There’s an electricity here that I’ve never felt before. You’ll see nearly 80,000 people literally shaking this structure. It’s going to be truly incredible.”

“It is. You know, when we bought the club four years ago, I started working on this right away. And part of what we’re trying to do here is bring the best of America to Italian soccer, and specifically to AC Milan.

“It’s extraordinary: most people didn’t think we could accomplish all this, or even get to this point. Not only did we announce the stadium this week, but we also announced the largest naming rights deal in history. I think it’s a wonderful thing for Italian soccer.

“The message to everyone is that we’re coming back. Italian soccer truly deserves to be at the top again, and we’ll do everything we can through AC Milan to make that happen.”

“There’s a connection between us. We’re two guys from Pennsylvania trying to make their way and understand Italy. He’s ‘Captain America’, but he’s also my franchise player. I couldn’t be more thrilled to have him with us.

“It’s a true partnership with him. He’s a leader, and his presence on the court makes all the difference in the world. You’ve seen it this year: when he steps on the court, he completely changes the game.”

Why is AC Milan and winning with Milan so important to you?

“I remember George Steinbrenner telling me about twenty years ago, ‘Don’t get too emotionally involved in all this; don’t go into the locker room, don’t do those things’. But in this case, I got emotionally involved.

“For me, it became a personal issue. And I don’t know what it is: maybe it’s just that… look around, listen. This means so much to the people of Milan, it has a huge impact on them, and I feel a huge responsibility.

“AC Milan were at the top of the world, and we’re going to bring them back there. This will probably be one of the greatest things I’ll do in my career.”

“It’s something multigenerational. This stadium is an icon. Look around, look at these people: this means everything to them. All of this carries with it an enormous amount of responsibility, it almost makes your wrists tremble.

“The best thing I can do is do my job well to help this club win, because victory spreads to the whole community. Listen… can you hear? That’s the Curva. You don’t see anything like it in America, it’s truly special.”

What’s your favourite detail of the new stadium?

“What we tried to do with the new stadium was to live up to the name and tradition of this facility. This is San Siro, but it is also ‘La Scala del Calcio’, the opera house of football.

“When you look at the design of the new stadium, those majestic, slender lines on the exterior recall the grand drapes and curtains of La Scala. It is our tribute to the history of this city.”

How did you manage to unblock the stadium situation?

“It’s been an incredible collaboration, a true partnership with our president Paolo Scaroni and the mayor. It’s extraordinary because everyone told me it’s impossible to build a stadium here in Italy. The last major stadium built in Italy was in 2011 in Turin, with about 40,000 seats.

“The extraordinary thing about our project is that it will be a 75,000-seat stadium, and we expect to have it completed before the 2032 European Championships. It will be a great thing for all of Italy.”

#2289. Pisa, Bianco: "La Serie A è un obiettivo, ma non deve diventare un'ossessione"

Paolo Bianco, tecnico del Pisa, ha parlato in conferenza stampa alla vigilia della sfida con il SudTirol

Bianco, come valuta le parole di Comolli sull’obiettivo Serie A e sulla possibilità di raggiungerlo già quest’anno?

"Credo che il Pisa abbia ricevuto troppi complimenti dopo le partite contro Verona, Cremonese e Palermo. Una squadra già pronta e matura porta a casa più punti, invece ci sono stati troppi complimenti. Non siamo la squadra da battere dopo il Palermo, così come abbiamo ricevuto troppe critiche dopo la sconfitta contro l’Avellino. Non abbiamo fatto una buona partita, ma non siamo andati così male come è emerso dai giudizi. I tifosi vogliono vedere la propria squadra giocare bene, ma ci sono anche gli avversari e bisogna accettare quello che fanno quando giocano contro di noi.

Sul presidente, sono stato il primo a parlare dopo il cambio societario e vi avevo detto che il livello si stava alzando velocemente. Quando si vuole alzare il livello, lo si vuole fare in ogni ambito. Se andremo in Serie A quest’anno non lo so, perché non ho la bacchetta magica, ma ci sono tutti i presupposti. L’obiettivo c’è, ma non deve diventare un’ossessione, perché non porta da nessuna parte".

Che partita si aspetta contro il SudTirol?

"Affrontiamo una squadra che è un po’ la sorpresa del campionato. Sappiamo naturalmente quali difficoltà ci attendono".

Ci sono diversi giocatori che hanno trovato meno spazio. Possono avere un’opportunità?

"Ci sono tanti giocatori che hanno giocato meno, non soltanto Canestrelli e Caracciolo, ma anche Coppola, Piccinini e Ferrah. Petagna, secondo me, sta molto bene. Spero che continuino a mettermi in difficoltà e c’è stata la crescita di alcuni di loro".

Confente è stato tra i più criticati. È possibile un cambio in porta?

"Non è un problema. È un portiere importante per la categoria, quello giusto per la squadra e per me".

Come sta la squadra dal punto di vista fisico?

"La squadra sta molto bene fisicamente. Quando affronti le formazioni più forti del campionato, le energie consumate sono sicuramente elevate. Bisogna tenerne conto, soprattutto quando ci sono più impegni settimanali. La settimana completa ci ha fatto bene".

Piccinini ha giocato tutte le 38 partite dello scorso campionato, ma quest’anno fatica a trovare spazio. Come mai?

"Sono più in difficoltà io che lui, perché in allenamento è perfetto. Deve soltanto continuare ad allenarsi. Nessuno poteva pensare che Primasso fosse titolare in questa squadra, ma quando vedo i giocatori migliorare devo impiegarli. Piccinini avrà modo di ritagliarsi il suo spazio. Troppo spesso quest’anno ho dovuto sostituire giocatori per scelta".

Come si può scardinare la difesa del SudTirol? Petagna e Pittarello possono giocare insieme?

"Il SudTirol ha cambiato sia direttore sia allenatore. Lovisa e Possanzini sono due ragazzi importanti, che sanno fare il loro lavoro al meglio. Affrontiamo una squadra organizzata, che si difende con il 5-4-1 sia in blocco basso sia più alta e che gioca bene a calcio. Dovremo essere molto equilibrati. Contro l’Avellino siamo stati bravi a riempire l’area nell’ultima mezz’ora, meno nella prima ora. Quest’anno abbiamo portato la palla in attacco 95 volte".

Contro l’Avellino il Pisa è stato impreciso sotto porta. Come se lo spiega?

"Sono d’accordo. È paradossale che nella partita in cui ci siamo avvicinati di più all’area in campionato abbiamo prodotto così poco."

Marras ha segnato tre gol in tre partite, ma è stato schierato titolare soltanto due volte. Può partire dall’inizio?

"Tanti giocatori, come dicevo, meriterebbero di giocare dal primo minuto. Marras ha fatto tre gol in tre partite ed è devastante. È un giocatore che spacca le partite, come dice Possanzini".

Marras può essere impiegato ancora come quinto di destra?

"Ormai avete visto che quando ho bisogno lo tiro fuori dal mazzo. Può suonare e cantare ovunque". Le sue parole riportate da Sestaporta.news.

#2288. RB Leipzig vs. Man City lineups confirmed: Captain Haaland as Cherki, Foden, Guehi decisions made for Champions League game

By Oliver Thomas | 04 Nov 2026 18:52, Last updated: 04 Nov 2026 19:11

Erling Haaland will captain Manchester City in tonight’s Champions League clash against RB Leipzig at the Red Bull Arena.

The prolific Norwegian drew a blank in front of goal in City’s 1-0 Premier League home defeat to Brighton last weekend, but he has scored more goals against Leipzig (12 in just eight games) than he has against any other opponent in his career and will be looking to add to his tally in Germany.

Manager Enzo Maresca has made a total of four changes to his starting lineup, with Marc Guehi, Nico O'Reilly, Rayan Cherki and Phil Foden all recalled at the expense of Ruben Dias, Josko Gvardiol, Ayyoub Bouaddi and Jeremy Doku.

Haaland will wear the captain’s armband in the absence of Dias, who will begin on the substitutes’ bench for the first time this season in either the Champions League or Premier League.

Guehi and Abdukodir Khusanov will therefore start together at centre-back, with Matheus Nunes and O’Reilly playing either side of them as full-backs.

Elliot Anderson and Enzo Fernandez retain their midfield partnership, with Cherki returning to operate in the number 10 role as Foden and Iliman Ndiaye begin out wide in support of Haaland leading the line.

As for Leipzig, head coach Martin Demichelis – a former Man City defender – has made just two changes to the side that began the 2-0 win over Schalke 04 in the Bundesliga last weekend.

Christoph Baumgartner is fit to start in centre-midfield alongside Neil El Aynaoui and Ezechiel Banzuzi, with the Austrian coming into the first XI at the expense of Rocco Reitz.

The other change sees Ridle Baku replace Benjamin Henrichs at right-back, joining Willi Orban, Castello Lukeba and David Raum in a four-man defence.

Antonio Nusa and Romulo Cardoso, who were both on target against Schalke, continue in a three-man frontline next to Brighton loanee Brajan Gruda.

Christopher Nkunku, who scored a memorable UCL hat-trick against Man City in a 6-3 defeat at the Etihad in 2021-22, will begin as a substitute along with defender Maxime Esteve, who has recovered from a knock to make the matchday squad.

RB Leipzig starting lineup: Vandevoordt; Baku, Orban, Lukeba, Raum; Baumgartner, Banzuzi, El Aynaoui; Gruda, Romulo, Nusa

Subs: Nyland, Kone, Esteve, Klostermann, Henrichs, Seiwald, Reitz, Maksimovic, Bakayoko, Gomis, Nkunku

Manchester City starting lineup: Donnarumma; Nunes, Khusanov, Guehi, O’Reilly; Anderson, Fernandez; Ndiaye, Cherki, Foden; Haaland

Subs: Bettinelli, Rulli, Dias, Kovacic, Doku, Ait-Nouri, Reis, Gvardiol, Bouaddi, Allan, Samba, Lewis

#2287. Liverpool lead twice before losing to Fenerbahce in Champions League

Even Liverpool’s multi-million pound bench could not rescue a team floundering in Istanbul as a chaotic performance was punished by Fenerbahce as they twice came from behind to win 3-2.

Alexander Isak had scored within three minutes of coming on to put the visitors ahead going into the final quarter but, like in the first half, the lead lasted just four minutes before former West Brom trainee Archie Brown equalised courtesy of some lax defending.

But worse was to follow as Vedat Muriqi outjumped three players to nod in the winner and end Liverpool’s 100 per cent record.

And while Andoni Iraola will be scrutinised for making five changes, including resting three of his front four in Sunday’s defeat to Arsenal, it was the way the team lost their cool and control in a game they had in hand which was more damaging.

It was a harsh result on 19-year-old Trey Nyoni, who belied his relative inexperience with a composed performance capped by an excellent volley to open the scoring, becoming Liverpool’s youngest player to register his maiden goal in the Champions League and second-youngest scorer in the competition after Trent Alexander-Arnold.

But Liverpool’s Turkish troubles continued – they lost twice to Galatasaray last season – on a typically fractious night in Istanbul, with former Manchester United forward Mason Greenwood scoring the first equaliser with a free-kick which deflected in off Jeremy Jacquet.

They had been in total command up to that point but from there the game descended into something more manic, which suited a team hammered 6-0 at Villa Park last month and stoked a febrile home crowd.

Hearts get bad news on Calvin Miller in latest injury blow ‘I hold my hands up’ - McAvennie issues fresh apology to Celtic star Auston Trusty

Many of Liverpool’s players did not cope well in that atmosphere but, to his immense credit, Nyoni shone.

Iraola’s choice to leave out Isak, Florian Wirtz and Cody Gakpo as they come to the end of a run of nine games in 27 days was understandable but it left his side toothless.

Bradley Barcola was deployed as striker but his unfamiliarity with the role was evident after failing to properly anticipate Joe Gomez’s low cross to the near post.

He did flick one header wide but struggled with the physicality of Fenerbahce’s centre-backs, even though Kojo Oppong was making his first Champions League start.

There was a lot of naivety within the visitors’ ranks – Victor Munoz snatched at an early chance when he was found in acres of space before redeeming himself by eventually forcing Ederson into a save – but none of it came from Nyoni.

The teenager was composed on just his seventh Champions League appearance – with everything going through him at the base of midfield.

His use of the ball, tactical awareness and unshakeable belief in his own ability meant amid the hurly burly of a typically Turkish clash he was head and shoulders above everyone else.

Even the usually unflappable Virgil van Dijk’s patience was tested, pushing Irfan Kahveci to the floor at a second-half corner, having twice had Romelu Lukaku leave something on him in the early stages.

When Liverpool’s breakthrough came it was fully deserved as Rio Ngumoha’s cross to the far post saw Van Dijk’s pressure force Brown to head weakly to the edge of the penalty area where Nyoni dispatched the dropping ball via a deflection off Oppong.

But after a sliding Kahveci smashed against the crossbar from five yards, Greenwood’s free-kick went through the wall.

Isak headed in Gakpo’s cross with his first chance after those two and Wirtz were summoned in the 66th minute, but the unmarked Brown tapped in the equaliser and more poor defending saw Muriqi head home the winner.

#2286. Joao Neves rules out Premier League move in PSG pledge

By Naim Beneddra | 04 Nov 2026 18:52

Paris Saint-Germain midfielder Joao Neves has delivered a clear message about his future, revealing that he has no ambition to play in the Premier League or La Liga despite previously attracting interest from some of Europe's biggest clubs.

The 22-year-old Portugal international has established himself as a key figure in Luis Enrique's side since arriving from Benfica in 2024, helping the French champions secure back-to-back Champions League titles.

However, Neves has now gone a step further than simply expressing his satisfaction with life in Paris, revealing that he would like to spend the remainder of his playing career at the Parc des Princes.

His comments come shortly after signing a new contract until June 2031 and ahead of PSG's continued pursuit of a third successive European crown.

The midfielder also underlined his importance on the pitch on Tuesday evening, opening the scoring and providing an assist in PSG's emphatic 6-0 Champions League victory away to Villarreal.

Speaking in an interview with French broadcaster CANAL+, Neves made it clear that moving to either England or Spain is not among his career objectives.

"I don't have the objective of going to Spain or England," the former Benfica youngster explained.

"I have an objective, and it is to feel good in my life and to win here. Joao wants to stay here his whole career."

The declaration is particularly significant given Neves's age and the transfer speculation that has surrounded him since his emergence in Portuguese football.

Manchester United were among the clubs linked with the midfielder before his move to PSG, while Manchester City were subsequently credited with an interest in bringing him to the Etihad Stadium.

In September 2025, reports suggested that City were considering an offer worth approximately £105m for Neves, although PSG were understood to be unwilling to entertain his departure.

That speculation never developed into a confirmed transfer agreement, and there is no indication that either Manchester club is currently negotiating for his signature.

Nevertheless, Neves's latest comments suggest that even the prospect of playing in England's top flight holds little appeal to him at present.

His position is supported by his recent contractual commitment.

In September, PSG announced a series of extensions involving several important members of Luis Enrique's squad, including Neves, Willian Pacho, Lucas Beraldo, Senny Mayulu and Fabian Ruiz.

The Portuguese midfielder's agreement now runs until the summer of 2031, giving the French champions considerable security over his future.

While such a contract does not rule out a transfer later in his career, Neves has made his personal preference unusually clear.

For Premier League clubs hoping to attract one of Europe's most accomplished young midfielders, his comments provide little encouragement.

Neves's commitment to PSG is closely connected to the success he has experienced since leaving Benfica.

The Portugal international has developed into an integral part of Luis Enrique's midfield alongside compatriot Vitinha, combining an ability to regain possession with the technical quality required to operate in tight spaces.

His contribution has been particularly important to PSG's recent European dominance.

Since his arrival in 2024, the French club have won two consecutive Champions League titles, establishing themselves among the continent's leading teams.

Neves has also demonstrated that his role extends beyond winning possession and maintaining control in midfield.

Against Villarreal on Tuesday, he opened the scoring after nine minutes, reacting quickly inside the penalty area following a corner.

He later supplied the assist for David Boly's goal as PSG completed their record-breaking away victory in the Champions League.

The result maintained the defending champions' perfect record in this season's league phase, with four victories from their opening four matches.

Despite that impressive start, Neves believes maintaining the standards established over the previous two seasons will present a considerable challenge.

"It's the mentality," he explained when discussing PSG's continued ambition.

"You have two Champions Leagues, but you keep making the necessary efforts. Maintaining that quality is even harder than winning two Champions Leagues."

The midfielder also identified a specific objective for the current European campaign.

"We want to try to finish in the top eight, something we haven't managed in the last two seasons."

That target reflects PSG's determination to avoid the additional knockout-round play-offs and secure direct qualification for the last 16.

Neves's performances against Villarreal offered another illustration of why Luis Enrique considers him central to those ambitions.

And while his contract already ties him to Paris for several more years, his latest declaration suggests that he sees no need to leave the French capital to fulfil his remaining ambitions in football.

#2285. Virtus Verona 2-3 Milan Futuro: Menon, Perera and Pagliei score in entertaining win

Milan Futuro are back in the win column as they managed to secure a thrilling 3-2 victory away against Virtus Verona.

Milan Futuro battled hard against Pavonese in the rearranged Serie D matchday six fixture on Wednesday night and got a 1-1 draw away from home, despite having gone down to 10 men. It was a result to build on, and build on it they did.

Read SempreMilan ad-free and get access to exclusive news. Click here for a free trial!

After a tentative start in which both teams had a few half-chances, it was the home team took the lead in the 20th minute with a shot from outside the box by Cuel that deceived Pittarella.

The Rossoneri equalised in the 22nd minute through Luca Menon, who beat the opposing goalkeeper with a powerful left-footed shot from the edge of the box. After the equaliser, both teams had opportunities to go ahead, but it was level at the break.

The Rossoneri got off to a much better start in the second half, taking the lead in the 49th minute after a persistent attack through Nirash Perera, who volleyed in a fine cross from the right.

Navarro’s team then made it 3-1 in the 73rd minute with Matteo Pagliei, who headed in a great Lorenzo Ossola cross. The home side made it a one-goal game again in the 78th minute through Franchi, but it wasn’t enough.

The Futuro therefore return to winning ways in the league and climbs to 17 points in the standings, with the second half of the match against Piacenza still to be played after that was suspended due to the referee getting injured.

#2284. Torino, Abate: "Da sosta a sosta siamo cambiati: strada giusta, Frosinone non è passo indietro"

14.15 - Ignazio Abate presenta Torino-Lecce, gara valida per la undicesima giornata del campionato di Serie A in programma domani alle 15 al Grande Torino. Il tecnico granata incontrerà i giornalisti nella sala conferenze dello stadio Olimpico Grande Torino, segui la diretta testuale su Tuttomercatoweb.com. L'inizio della conferenza è previsto per le 14.30.

Ore 14.30 - Comincia la conferenza stampa di Abate

Il Lecce chiude un periodo significativo con crescita e risultati: quanto voglia di riscatto c'è? "Se pensiamo alla sosta scorsa, ci arriviamo in un altro modo. Significa che la strada è giusta, poi se penso alle ultime settimane Frosinone non l'ho vissuto come passo indietro. Non siamo una squadra evoluta, servono anche giri a vuoto, ma anche a Frosinone ho visto cose positive. Non possiamo farci trasportare dalle emotività, bisogna essere lucidi e obiettivi. Ad oggi siamo una squadra che deve crescere nelle letture e in alcuni momenti, a Frosinone primo tempo di livello contro una squadra forte e che ha altissima intensità di pressing, oltre ad aver segnato tanto nei primi 45 minuti e noi eravamo 0-0"

Quali sono i correttivi per domani? "Le cose si aggiustano con il tempo, si cresce con il tempo. Ci manca un po' di maturità in alcuni momenti della gara: a Frosinone siamo mancati nell'ultimo passaggio, non bisogna prendere gol su palla inattiva dopo due minuti. Siamo andati dietro all'inerzia di gara, ma noi crediamo in qualcosa e per trovare continuità il pensiero deve diventare più duro. Non possiamo lasciare spazio a dubbi. C'è margine di squadra, vedo una squadra accesa e sono convinto che faremo una partita giusta contro il Lecce che è difficile da affrontare. Per noi è complicata, tutti si aspettano di vincere ma non è scontato. Loro sono forti sulle palle inattive, ci vorrà un grande Torino"

Come sta Bragança? E se non dovesse giocare, il regista sarebbe Ilkhan o Fitz-Jim? "L'ho visto bene, ci sono stati giorni per recuperare. In quella posizione vedo Ilkhan, gli piace stare nel vivo del gioco e può darci equilibrio"

Come stanno Belghali e Cacciamani? "Hanno acciacchi, ma con la gara prima della sosta serve stringere i denti senza fasciarsi la testa. Mi hanno dato disponibilità, vedremo chi partirà dall'inizio"

Rodriguez è stato convocato dalla Svizzera: come sta? "Verranno fatte valutazioni, gli manca l'ultimo step per il recupero. Stanno valutando con la società"

Sette gol subiti negli ultimi 15 minuti: la preoccupa? "E' un dato che ho analizzato, con l'aumento del tempo effettivo calano i ritmi e vediamo più gol nel campionato. Ma noi dobbiamo migliorare, è uno degli step di maturità. A Frosinone abbiamo preso gol ad inizio secondo tempo e poi nel recupero, col Como pure, dobbiamo crescere"

Come sta Zapata? "Lavora, lo vedo in crescita e mi auguro che migliori ancora"

Sulle sconfitte pesano di più gli infortuni o una fragilità caratteriale? "Non vado dietro all'alibi degli infortuni, ho una rosa che mi permette di competere. Fragilità? Abbiamo metri di giudizio diversi, io questa fragilità non la vedo e non mi faccio trasportare dalla vostra emotività. A Frosinone i tre dietro hanno fatto roba vera, il salvataggio di Coco è roba vera. Vedo spirito e anima disumana, abbiamo fatto un gran secondo tempo in fase difensiva. Fa parte del percorso, dobbiamo crescere in alcuni momenti della partita. Ogni week-end c'è una gara completamente diversa l'una dall'altra. Non dobbiamo farci trascinare dall'inerzia delle partite, è uno step ancora da fare"

Come mai un calo realizzativo? "Forse è un po' eccessivo...Non possiamo passare dal grande entusiasmo ad essere disfattisti. Per me non è svanito l'entusiasmo, lo percepisco in spogliatoio e dalla risposta dei tifosi. Ci sono cadute, ma vogliamo finire nel modo giusto e vincere domani. Pensiamo alle cose di campo: se non segnano gli attaccanti, speriamo facciano gol gli altri"

Tre convocati in U21 azzurra "Ci crediamo. Mi auguro e spero che qualche ragazzo possa anche assaporare la magia della Nazionale maggiore, ma tutto passa da qua dentro e dal lavoro nel Toro. Lo sanno bene, ma pensiamo a domani"

Cosa vuole vedere domani dal suo Toro? "La lettura dei momenti della gara è legata al tempo, ci vuole tempo per raggiungere la maturità totale. Bisogna vincere le partite sporche, e domani voglio una squadra solida cercando di giocare a calcio. Non dipenderà solo da noi, anche dagli avversari e dal tipo di gara che sarà"

Quanto sente sua questa città? Ha visto musei torinesi? "Mi dispiacere deludervi, i miei posti preferiti sono il Filadelfia e lo stadio. Non sono un marito semplice, mia moglie è tosta e mi sopporta ma vivo completamente per il mio lavoro, per i miei ragazzi e per la mia squadra. Sentimento e ambizione di poter fare bene e regalare emozioni a questa gente va al di là, penso che più tempo dedichi alla causa più puoi riuscirci. E' la stessa mentalità che cerco di trasferire ai miei ragazzi, è solo così che si va oltre ai propri limiti"

Come sta Simeone? E quando rivedremo Casadei? "Stanno tutti bene. Casadei? Lo vedo bene, migliora ma come tutti. Mi aspetto tanto da tutti. Fuori dai convocati Fortini e Rodriguez, Mandragora lo valutiamo oggi pomeriggio"

Che consigli dà ad Adams? "Sono molto legato a lui, è un ragazzo giusto e di qualità. A lui e agli altri dico che non possiamo cullarci su ciò che abbiamo fatto, ma bisogna lavorare per migliorarsi. Me lo porto dietro dalla mia carriera. Adams ha fatto un inizio di campionato importante, ma può andare in doppia cifra e deve pretenderlo da se stesso. Può regalarci soddisfazioni"

Domani chi gioca a centrocampo? "Ne dico uno: gioca Fitz-Jim"

Mezzo secolo senza il Capitano dei Capitani, Ferrini: quanto ha senso buttare in spogliatoio anche questo seme? O magari non verrebbe raccolto? "Non mi piace essere ipocrita. Ciò che siamo noi, con il massimo rispetto della storia che non è facile inculcarla nei ragazzi stranieri, e noi vogliamo dimostrare ciò che siamo noi. Il pubblico ci tende una mano: lo dico con rispetto della storia, dobbiamo crescere cercando di vincere. Dobbiamo essere aggrappati ai punti. Distanza tra storia e società? Domenica mattina sarò lì al Filadelfia per la messa per Ferrini, ma dico che domani il risultato è troppo importante per noi. Con tutto il rispetto per la storia"

#2283. Hamburger SV vs Borussia Monchengladbach - prediction, team news, lineups

By Lewis Nolan | 04 Nov 2026 19:16, Last updated: 05 Nov 2026 01:35

Volksparkstadion will be the backdrop for an early-season six-pointer in the Bundesliga's relegation battle on Friday, when Hamburger SV host Borussia Monchengladbach.

HSV find themselves in 15th place with just nine points after eight matchweeks, though their position is not nearly as dire as the visitors, who are last in 18th place with four points.

Hamburger should not yet panic about their position in the table give just two points separate them from eighth-placed Eintracht Frankfurt, but they have lost key matches to rivals in recent weeks.

The hosts' last two Bundesliga games were 1-0 and 3-1 losses against Paderborn and Frankfurt respectively, the latter of which occurred on November 1.

Between those two results was another 3-1 defeat against Frankfurt in the DFB-Pokal, but it should be noted that they had won their prior three matches, with all three successes coming in the top flight.

Merlin Polzin will be concerned that only one team have conceded more times in the Bundesliga this season (19), while his players have only netted two goals in their past three league outings.

HSV's form at home has been strong considering they have won two of their three clashes at Volksparkstadion in the top flight this term, and a win on Friday would be their third consecutive victory in the division at the stadium.

Gladbach may be at the foot of the table, but they have now taken four points from the last six on offer, including when they beat Stuttgart 3-2 on October 23.

Die Fohlen are unsurprisingly the worst defensive team in the division having conceded 22 times in just eight games, though perhaps their goalless stalemate with Paderborn on October 31 is a sign of improvement.

Boss Alexander Blessin was widely praised for his time at St Pauli, especially for 2024-25, when his team ended the campaign with the second fewest number of goals conceded (41).

The visitors' record against their hosts has not been positive considering they have only won one of their five most recent meetings with HSV, losing on two occasions while failing to win any of their last three encounters with Hamburger.

A victory for Gladbach would be their second in a row on the road following their success against Stuttgart, though they did lose their opening three away Bundesliga fixtures.

Borussia Monchengladbach form (all competitions):

Hamburger still have a number of injury concerns to deal with, including to centre-backs Sebastiaan Bornauw (jaw) and Nicolas Capaldo (muscle), while striker Yussuf Poulsen's hip issue is likely to keep him sidelined.

Whether Albert Gronbaek can lead the line and score his first Bundesliga goal of the season remains to be seen, though he will need support from midfielders Albert Sambi Lokonga, Miro Muheim and Martin Adeline.

In defence, expect to see a three-man unit consisting of Shafiq Nandja, Jordan Torunarigha and Warmed Omari.

Gladbach come into the clash without the likes of Hugo Bolin (muscle), Franck Honorat (muscle) and Tim Kleindienst, leaving their attack looking especially light.

Isac Lidberg may be trusted to start as his team's number nine, while Robin Hack and Wael Mohya could play in supportive roles.

Hamburger's form has been disappointing, but given they have often played well at home this season, they are sure to pose a challenge on Friday.

#2281. Celtic boss Martin O'Neill responds to Frank McAvennie's Auston Trusty remarks

Celtic manager Martin O'Neill has criticised comments made by former Parkhead forward Frank McAvennie about Auston Trusty following the sad passing of his father.

McAvennie questioned the amount of time the American had taken off to celebrate the life of his dad, before apologising via a statement on X.

Speaking ahead of Celtic's Europa League clash with Besiktas, O'Neill responded to McAvennie's initial remarks.

The 74-year-old explained that Trusty had requested leave and that he is with the defender 'the whole way'.

"That's really poor taste," O'Neill said of McAvennie's comments. "I know I've said some things and ended up backtracking. But that's a family bereavement.

"And that's making someone's mind up about how long they should be grieving and stuff like this here. That's not on.

"Trusty asked me for some time [off]. Even if it's a celebration [of his late father’s life], it doesn't matter. The father's been in poor health for quite some considerable time.

"And I know there was a stage where he thought that he wasn't going to see it through. But I'm with Trusty the whole way there. Because I wouldn't want anybody to tell me that I had to grieve over a brother or sister dying. And I've had that.

"He's 28 years of age. And he's taken it badly. Honestly. I've afforded him all the time he wants.

"At some stage, obviously, he'll be coming back. But at the end of the day, I've given that time because I know that we might be talking about different eras or whatever the case may be. That's nothing to do with it. This is a family thing. And that's not right.

"So, Trusty has got the time and he's got the backing of myself and he's got the backing of the club."
